	jQuery(function($) {
		
		// Mobile Nav Home Top Layer Accordion
		var allHomeMobileNavPanels = $('.home-mobile-nav .accordion-mobile-nav > dd').hide(); // Hide panels
		$('.home-mobile-nav .accordion-mobile-nav > dt > a').click(function() {
			
			$this = jQuery(this);
			$target = $this.parent().next();
			
			if ($target.hasClass('active')) {
			  $(this).parent().removeClass('active');
			  $target.removeClass('active').slideUp();
			  $(this).removeClass('minus');
			
			} else {
			  $target.addClass('active').slideDown();
			  $(this).parent().addClass('active');
			  $(this).addClass('minus');
			}    
			return false;
		
		});
		
		// Mobile Nav Home Second Layer Accordion
		var allHomeMobileNavLayer2Panels = $('.home-mobile-nav .accordion-nav-index > dd').removeClass('active').hide(); // Hide panels
		$('.home-mobile-nav .accordion-nav-index > dt > a.activate').click(function() {
			
			$('.minus').removeClass('minus');
			$this = $(this);
			$target = $this.parent().next();
			
			if ($target.hasClass('active')) {
			  $target.removeClass('active').slideUp();
			
			} else {
				allHomeMobileNavLayer2Panels.removeClass('active').slideUp();
			  $target.addClass('active').slideDown();
			  $(this).addClass('minus');
			}    
			return false;
		
		});
		
	});
</script>
